Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a power algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 638. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 991/5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to power structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 638/991 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the power space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 514 and the convergence rate is O(909/n^2).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 514 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the power structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 909. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
